- **mrr-arr-snapshot**: Computes current MRR, ARR, and revenue breakdown from active Stripe subscriptions — by plan, interval, and currency. - **ms1-feature-correlation-clustering**: Use when you have an unclustered MS1 feature table with per-sample intensities (e.g., ExampleDatasets/README/df_test_pos_not_clustered.csv) and need to consolidate features from the same metabolite into single rows before ipaPy2 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-correlation-clustering))
- **ms1-feature-detection-and-annotation**: Use when you have FIA-MS, LC-MS, or GC-MS full-scan data in mzML format and need to identify unknown molecular features by accurate mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-feature-detection-and-annotation) · 4 copies)
- **ms1-feature-extraction**: Use when when you have an LC-HRMS feature table (with m/z values, retention times, and isotopic signatures) and a suspect compound database (with reference m/z, expected retention time windows, isotope ratios, and neutral loss fragments), and you need to rapidly prioritize which features are most. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **ms1-feature-ion-species-annotation**: Use when an aligned feature list from LC-MS/MS metabolomics must be grouped into ion species (adducts, isotopes, multimers) per molecule, or when relative ion-formation percentages per adduct must be computed and compared across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-ion-species-annotation))
- **ms1-feature-metabolite-mass-matching**: Use when starting MetDNA3/MrnAnnoAlgo3 annotation: you have a curated MRN (igraph), an MS1 feature table, and seed metabolite annotations, and must constrain the network before reaction mapping and MS2-similarity propagation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-metabolite-mass-matching))
- **ms1-feature-peak-detection-in-full-scan**: Use when you have merged MS1 spectra (output from spectral binning/merging steps) from a full-scan FIA-MS or LC-MS acquisition and need to identify distinct molecular features before accurate mass annotation or background filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-feature-peak-detection-in-full-scan) · 3 copies)
- **ms1-feature-ranking-and-extraction**: Use when when you have raw LC-MS data files and need to identify which compounds were actually detected at high abundance during a gradient run, prior to evaluating whether the gradient provided good separation across the chemical space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-feature-ranking-and-extraction) · 3 copies)
- **ms1-feature-relationship-annotation**: Use when you have a clustered MS1 feature table (pandas dataframe with ids, rel.ids, mzs, RTs, Int columns) and need to collapse isotope peaks into isotope pattern units before adduct computation or IPA annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-relationship-annotation))
- **ms1-feature-table-processing**: Use when a raw vendor MS1 feature table (sample plus ≥3 solvent-blank feature tables) must be de-noised prior to exact mass matching, mass defect filtering, or homologous-series detection for PFAS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-table-processing))
- **ms1-feature-table-qc-summarization**: Use when a pre-processed LC-MS/MS peak/feature table (e.g., Progenesis export such as PTY087I2) plus sample metadata must be QC-filtered and summarized into per-filter pass/fail ion counts before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-feature-table-qc-summarization))
- **ms1-full-scan-acquisition-simulation**: Use when when you need to prototype, test, or benchmark MS1-only acquisition strategies on a defined set of metabolites (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-full-scan-acquisition-simulation) · 3 copies)
- **ms1-ion-abundance-ranking**: Use when a QC batch in PeakQC lacks user-specified target ions and you need the software to pick abundant, frequently detected MS1 ions per sample group as auto-tracked QC targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ion-abundance-ranking))
- **ms1-ion-feature-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw mzLearn output (a feature table of median rt/m/z-defined signals with normalized intensities) must be standardized and split into X_Pretrain_*/X_Finetune_* CSVs before VAE training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ion-feature-matrix-preprocessing))
- **ms1-low-collision-energy-filtering**: Use when computing TIC, EIC, or MS1 spectra from arcMS-converted Parquet files containing both low and high energy (HDMSE) scans and only precursor (MS1) signal should be aggregated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-low-collision-energy-filtering))
- **ms1-ms2-database-integration**: Use when building or extending a plant metabolomics annotation pipeline that needs both MS1 exact-mass tables and MS2 spectral libraries merged into a single search database, e.g. for MetMiner/metID-based compound annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ms2-database-integration))
- **ms1-ms2-feature-correspondence**: Use when processing raw LC–MS/MS runs where per-sample MS1 peaks must be linked to their acquired MS2 spectra before any clustering, spectral merging, or bioactivity correlation — the article's Preprocessing step extracts MS2 spectra and determines MS1–MS2 correspondence per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ms2-feature-correspondence))
- **ms1-ms2-feature-result-merging**: Use when you have SIRIUS (.json) outputs and spectral dereplication results (spectral_results.csv) for .mzML-derived features and need to combine them with the MS1DATA.csv feature table to obtain ranked final candidates per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ms2-feature-result-merging))
- **ms1-ms2-median-ratio-analysis**: Use when comparing HeLa Pierce QC RAW runs to a reference set to diagnose whether peptide-identification loss stems from MS1 TIC decline or an MS2 isolation/fragment-transmission failure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-ms2-median-ratio-analysis))
- **ms1-peak-deconvolution**: Use when processing LC/HRMS untargeted metabolomics datasets where peaks lack MS2 spectra or available MS2 libraries poorly cover the chemical space, and chemical annotation must be recovered from MS1-only data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-peak-deconvolution))
- **ms1-peak-intensity-extraction**: Use when high-resolution MS/MS or MS1 data need precursor and isotope peak abundances extracted after preprocessing, as input to isotope pattern-based molecular formula deduction (e.g. SIRIUS). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-peak-intensity-extraction))
- **ms1-precursor-candidate-screening**: Use when annotating tandem MS spectra and you need the initial MS1 precursor m/z candidate screen (e.g. 20 ppm delta_mz window) against a compound database in HDF5 format, or when measuring candidates per peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-precursor-candidate-screening))
- **ms1-scan-extraction-and-filtering**: Use when you have a Thermo Fisher Scientific .raw file from an Orbitrap instrument and need to programmatically retrieve MS1 spectral attributes (base-peak m/z, intensity, retention time) for downstream statistical analysis or quality control in R, rather than relying on external preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-scan-extraction-and-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **ms1-scan-filtering**: Use when the goal is to restrict spectral access to MS1-level scans of a Thermo Fisher .raw file (e.g. building a base-peak table, TIC, or XIC) and the input is a proprietary Orbitrap raw file readable by rawR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-scan-filtering))
- **ms1-scan-priority-ranking-construction**: Use when an mzML full-scan dataset plus a prior TopN MS1/MS2 scan schedule must be turned into a per-scan ranked MS2 fragmentation priority list via weighted (nonbinary) ion intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-scan-priority-ranking-construction))
- **ms1-signal-detection-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw LC/MS data in mzML format needs automated MS1 peak detection and alignment without user-set parameters or QC samples, or when benchmarking signal counts/TP-FP rates against XCMS/ASARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-signal-detection-pipeline-execution))
- **ms1-spectral-annotation-chemical-space**: Use when you have (1) a peaklist from untargeted LC/HRMS analysis with m/z and intensity values (typically output from IDSL.IPA or similar peak-picking tools), (2) a need to assign molecular formulas to detected peaks, and (3) only MS1 spectral data available (MS/MS is not required). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-spectral-annotation-chemical-space) · 2 copies)
- **ms1-spectrum-extraction**: Use when validating a ZODIAC/SIRIUS-annotated molecular formula for a single LC-MS feature by comparing its measured MS1 isotope pattern (M, M+1, M+2, ...) against a simulated pattern from the elemental composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-spectrum-extraction))
- **ms1-spectrum-simulation**: Use when when you need to generate synthetic LC-MS/MS data to test fragmentation strategies, validate acquisition controllers, or benchmark peak-picking and spectral matching algorithms before deployment on real mass spectrometers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-spectrum-simulation) · 2 copies)
- **ms1-spectrum-subsetting**: Use when after generating a scan index from a Thermo Fisher Orbitrap raw file using readIndex(), apply this skill when your analysis goal requires working exclusively with MS1 (precursor) scans rather than tandem MS (MS2/MS3) spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms1-spectrum-subsetting) · 2 copies)
- **ms1-time-mz-image-generation**: Use when raw MS files (Agilent 'd', Thermo '.raw', Bruker 'd', or mzML) must be screened for global batch quality via MS1-derived time-versus-m/z images and PCA, before or alongside targeted peak-metric QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-time-mz-image-generation))
- **ms1-time-mz-image-principal-component-analysis**: Use when you have a batch of MS runs (Thermo .raw, Agilent .d, Bruker .d, or mzML; LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, or direct infusion) and need an omics-agnostic, MS1-based global quality check to flag defective runs or group-separating trends before deeper metrics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-time-mz-image-principal-component-analysis))
- **ms1-time-vs-mz-image-rendering**: Use when assessing global quality of a batch of untargeted MS runs (LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, direct infusion, DDA/DIA) before detailed peak-metric QC, and you need a fast, identification-free representation per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms1-time-vs-mz-image-rendering))
- **ms17-010-etrnlb-vuln**: MS17-010 (EternalBlue) is a critical vulnerability in Microsoft's SMBv1 implementation that allows remote code execution. Originally discovered by the NSA and leaked by the Shadow Brokers in 2017, it ([Undermybelt/hermes-skills](https://github.com/Undermybelt/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/red-teaming/anthropic-cybersecurity-skills/skills/ms17-010-etrnlb-vuln) · ★11)
- **ms17-SKILL.md**: &gt; MS17-010 (EternalBlue) is a critical vulnerability in Microsoft's SMBv1 implementation that allows remote code execution. Originally discovered by the NSA and leaked by the Shadow Brokers in 2017, it ([DCx7C5/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/DCx7C5/ai-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/vuln/exploit/ms17) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-adduct-base-peak-specification**: Use when extracting a layer-3 MS2-similarity subnetwork with retrieveSubNetwork3 and you must declare which peaks serve as base peaks and their adduct identities before merging subnetworks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-adduct-base-peak-specification))
- **ms2-annotation-evaluation**: Use when a new spectral library's practical value must be quantified as added annotations (unique matches, total count deltas) on a public LC-MS/MS dataset, e.g. drug-incubated bacterial cultures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-annotation-evaluation))
- **ms2-annotation-interpretation**: Use when after GNPS spectral library search has returned matched chemical annotations (with m/z values and cosine similarity scores) for MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-annotation-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-annotation-peak-count-restriction**: Use when you must quantify the marginal contribution of MS2 peak information to de novo formula annotation accuracy — e.g., reproducing an Np peak-ablation sweep or benchmarking MS1-only versus MS2-conditioned formula prediction on spectra with subformula annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-annotation-peak-count-restriction))
- **ms2-chimeric-spectrum-detection**: Use when processing DDA LC-MS/MS data where multiple centroids fall inside an MS2 isolation window and contamination-ion fragments must be removed from co-isolated chimeric spectra before compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-chimeric-spectrum-detection))
- **ms2-compound-annotation-filtering**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS data (DDA or DIA, with MS2 spectra from pooled QC samples) needs compound-level annotation with fragment-level evidence, and noisy/blank-contaminated features must be filtered before annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-compound-annotation-filtering))
- **ms2-cosine-similarity-networking**: Use when you have GNPS feature-based molecular networking spectral matches (mzML/mzXML-derived feature tables plus paired MS/MS spectra) and need a library-match-only chemical tree to compare sample chemistries against fingerprint-predicted or tree-agnostic metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-cosine-similarity-networking))
- **ms2-cosine-similarity-scoring**: Use when a user-provided tissue MS2 spectrum (e.g. exported from mzML) must be quantitatively compared against Met-ID's MS2 database of FMP-10-derivatized chemical standards and scored with cosine similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-cosine-similarity-scoring))
- **ms2-data-preprocessing-pipeline**: Use when you have raw MS2 spectra files (mzML, mgf, msp, mzxml) that may contain multiple MS2 spectra per feature and require reduction or standardization before library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-data-preprocessing-pipeline) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-dereplication**: Use when you have MS2 tandem mass spectrometry data in .mzML format and need to match unknown spectra against a reference library (GNPS, HMDB, MassBank) to identify which known compounds are present in your sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-dereplication) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-diagnostic-fragment-matching**: Use when you have centroided MS2 spectra from data-dependent LC- or GC-HRMS measurements and need to rapidly prioritize potential PFAS features within a larger feature set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-diagnostic-fragment-matching) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-embedding-generation**: Use when labeled 'relevant' and 'other' MS/MS spectra (e.g. MSnLib libraries, in-house BOKU database, wheat ear extract) must be converted into MS2DeepScore embeddings as classifier input features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-embedding-generation))
- **ms2-event-to-peak-coverage-classification**: Use when you have an mzML file containing MS2 fragmentation events plus picked-peak bounding boxes (m/z and RT min/max, e.g. exported from MZmine2) and need per-peak coverage flags to quantify fragmentation-controller coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-event-to-peak-coverage-classification))
- **ms2-filtered-functional-interpretation**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS data (DDA or SWATH-DIA mzML) have been processed to MS1 feature tables plus MS2 compound identifications, and the goal is to test whether MS2-informed functional analysis yields more significant pathways than MS1-only enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-filtered-functional-interpretation))
- **ms2-fingerprint-blank-contamination-removal**: Use when after aligning MS2 fingerprints across samples (generating a sample-by-feature occurrence matrix) and before applying visualization techniques (MDS/PCoA, TMAP, Heatmap) or statistical comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-blank-contamination-removal) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-fingerprint-distance-calculation**: Use when after MS2 fingerprints have been generated by counting MS2 peaks and neutral losses in each sample, and you have aligned them into a MemoMatrix (sample-by-fingerprint matrix). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-distance-calculation) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-fingerprint-distance-matrix-construction**: Use when samples were acquired under different LC methods (e.g. C18 vs C18 RT-shift) or show poor feature overlap, and you need RT-agnostic sample distances from MS2 spectra instead of an aligned feature table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-distance-matrix-construction))
- **ms2-fingerprint-generation**: Use when LC-MS/MS samples must be compared in a retention-time-agnostic way, e.g. across chemodiverse samples, different LC methods, or different mass spectrometers (Q-Exactive Orbitrap vs Maxis II Q-TOF), from MS2 peak lists such as MGF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-generation))
- **ms2-fingerprint-matrix-merging**: Use when you have two MemoMatrix objects generated from separate sample cohorts (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-matrix-merging) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-fingerprint-sample-metrics-interpretation**: Use when an LC-MS/MS dataset shows an acquisition-batch split (e.g., RT shift or MS sensitivity drift around a run date) and you must test whether MS2-fingerprint MEMO matrices mitigate it relative to feature-table PCoA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-sample-metrics-interpretation))
- **ms2-fingerprint-vector-generation**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS data in mzML, mzXML, or MGF format from one or more metabolomics samples and need to compare samples that may have poor overlap in detected features, strong retention time shifts between runs, or were acquired on different LC methods or MS technologies (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-vector-generation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-fingerprint-vectorization**: Use when metabolomics samples cannot be compared by RT-aligned features — chemodiverse samples with poor feature overlap, strong RT shifts, different LC gradients, or different MS instruments (e.g. Maxis Q-ToF vs Q-Exactive Orbitrap) — and a sample-level MS2 fingerprint matrix is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fingerprint-vectorization))
- **ms2-fragment-isotopologue-quantification**: Use when HRMS MS2 spectra are available for individual lipid isotopologues (e.g., M0 vs M6 of PC(36:4)) and acyl-chain-specific 13C labeling, not just precursor-level MID, is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-isotopologue-quantification))
- **ms2-fragment-m-z-grouping-and-aggregation**: Use when you have extracted MS/MS spectra for a given metabolomic feature across multiple replicates (e.g., after top-TIC filtering) and need to identify which fragments are reproducible across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fragment-m-z-grouping-and-aggregation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-fragment-mass-difference-matching**: Use when centroided ddMS2 mzML data (ideally one collision energy per precursor) and a completed FeatureFinding feature list are available, and features must be separated into PFAS-like analytes vs. matrix components using MS2 evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-mass-difference-matching))
- **ms2-fragment-one-to-one-matching**: Use when bridging prototype compounds to candidate metabolites from PATBS-processed LC-MS feature tables where MS1 mass-defect screening (±100 amu / ±25 mmu) is insufficient and MS2 fragment-level corroboration is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-one-to-one-matching))
- **ms2-fragment-pattern-recognition**: Use when you have data-dependent acquisition (DDA) MS2 spectra from HRMS measurements (ESI or APCI ionization) and need to prioritize potential PFAS features from a large pool of detected ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fragment-pattern-recognition) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-fragment-peak-annotation**: Use when you have an MS1-identified glycan–RT pair from LC-MS/MS (Orbitrap or MALDI-TOF) data in mzXML format and need to confirm the glycan identity by annotating its MS2 fragment peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-peak-annotation))
- **ms2-fragment-similarity-scoring**: Use when bridging in vivo metabolite features to in vitro TCM prototype components from LC-MS feature lists, and you need similarity-of-MS2-fragment evidence combined with m/z and mass-defect scoring to assign metabolites to prototypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-similarity-scoring))
- **ms2-fragment-spectrum-comparison**: Use when a m/z peak in a MALDI-MSI dataset (e.g., mouse brain imzML data acquired with DHB matrix) is suspected to be a matrix adduct of a known lipid, and a deposited on-tissue MS2 spectrum is available to verify the fragment-ion series against the parent ion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragment-spectrum-comparison))
- **ms2-fragmentation-event-to-peak-matching**: Use when raw mzML injections from a TopN DDA controller and full-scan mzML of the same serum (QCA) or beer (QCB) extracts are available, and you must measure what fraction of picked peaks received MS2 fragmentation events. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-fragmentation-event-to-peak-matching))
- **ms2-fragmentation-spectrum-parsing**: Use when you have raw MS2 spectra in common formats (mzML, mzXML, msp, MGF, JSON) and need to convert them into normalized, queryable spectral objects for downstream analysis such as MS2 fingerprinting, spectral similarity scoring, or sample comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-fragmentation-spectrum-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-metadata-harmonization**: Use when MS/MS library entries (e.g. GNPS .msp downloads) contain inconsistent or missing metadata — mismatched SMILES/InChI/InChIKey, salt-form annotations, molar-mass-vs-monoisotopic parent mass errors, or wrong adducts — and must be cleaned reproducibly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-metadata-harmonization))
- **ms2-molecular-formula-elucidation**: Use when MZmine 'Export for SIRIUS' .mgf files (feature MS1 isotopic patterns + MS2 spectra) need per-feature molecular formula and 2D structure candidates within a 10 ppm parent-mass tolerance, e.g. to later cross-check against ISDB-LOTUS spectral annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-molecular-formula-elucidation))
- **ms2-molecular-networking-annotation**: Use when you have LC-MS2 peak lists from a fractionated natural product extract and need to prioritize fractions for isolation or compare unknowns against reference compounds (e.g., swinholide A) via spectral similarity networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-molecular-networking-annotation))
- **ms2-ms3-fragment-peak-annotation**: Use when a LC-MS lipidomics pipeline has already produced MS2 and MS3+ spectral matches (e.g. from LipiDex 2 Spectrum Searcher) and fragment-level peak assignments are needed to validate or annotate chromatographic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-ms3-fragment-peak-annotation))
- **ms2-peak-detection-and-counting**: Use when you have raw MS2 spectral data (MGF, mzML, or msp format) and need to generate a sample-level fingerprint for comparison across metabolomics samples, especially when samples were acquired using different LC methods, mass spectrometer technologies, or exhibit poor feature overlap or large. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-peak-detection-and-counting) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-peak-intensity-normalization**: Use when replicate-grouped, deconvolved MS2 spectra (e.g., DDA or SWATH-DIA output) must be merged into one consensus spectrum per MS1 peak and fragment intensities need normalization before MSP export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-peak-intensity-normalization))
- **ms2-peak-list-parsing**: Use when input is an MS2 (or related peak list: dta, mgf, pkl, mzXML, mzData, mzML, PRIDE XML) file and you need one structured record per spectrum with header fields and a peak list, especially as spectra for mzIdentML-based analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/ms2-peak-list-parsing))
- **ms2-peak-mass-decomposition**: Use when annotating MS2 fragment peaks with chemical subformulae for a candidate precursor formula and you want to avoid costly fragmentation tree computation (e.g. as input features for formula scoring models like MIST-CF). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-peak-mass-decomposition))
- **ms2-peak-neutral-loss-counting**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS peak lists (e.g. .mgf) and need to vectorize samples without feature alignment — especially for chemodiverse samples with poor feature overlap or strong RT shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-peak-neutral-loss-counting))
- **ms2-precursor-coisolation-analysis**: Use when you have a simulated (e.g., SMITER-generated mzML) or real complex-mixture dataset and must estimate how many distinct peptides co-fragment within each MS2 spectrum before committing to a real MS experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-precursor-coisolation-analysis))
- **ms2-precursor-extraction**: Use when when you have a resolved spectrum file in mzML or mzXML format and need to identify where MS2 (tandem mass spectrometry) scans occur within an LC-MS run, particularly to annotate XIC displays with MS2 precursor positions or validate that MS2 events align with expected chromatographic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-precursor-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-product-ion-mass-difference-alignment**: Use when comparing a query MS/MS spectrum against reference library spectra to find structural analogues, and you need to explain which query–reference product-ion pairs (including mass-shifted ones like +15.99 Da) drive the similarity score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-product-ion-mass-difference-alignment))
- **ms2-recognition-feature-rescue**: Use when processing DDA LC-MS mzXML data in which conventional peak picking (XCMS.featureTable with snthresh=6, prefilter=c(3,100)) has already run but low-intensity or poorly shaped peaks with detectable MS2 spectra were missed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-recognition-feature-rescue))
- **ms2-representative-spectra-selection**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS mzML files contain multiple MS2 scans per chromatographic feature and you need one representative MS2 spectrum per feature (m/z, RT) to feed formula assignment or ML-based annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-representative-spectra-selection))
- **ms2-scan-content-quality-checking**: Use when a vendor-format raw LC-MS/MS file (e.g., from GNPS-MassIVE dataset MSV000083437) has been converted to .mzXML or .mzML and must be confirmed readable and MS2-scan-containing before molecular networking upload. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-scan-content-quality-checking))
- **ms2-scan-parameter-scheduling**: Use when you need to simulate or prototype a Top-N DDA acquisition method in ViMMS 2.0 — i.e. you have simulated chemicals or mzML-derived ROIs and must decide which MS1 precursors get fragmented in which MS2 scans at each point in the run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-scan-parameter-scheduling))
- **ms2-scan-proportion-computation**: Use when QC-comparing the MS1/MS2 acquisition balance across Thermo RAW runs — e.g., diagnosing underperforming HeLa QC runs from MetaXtract scan-header CSVs where only the Scan Mode column is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-scan-proportion-computation))
- **ms2-scan-to-feature-association**: Use when you have LC/MS data with detected molecular features (feature table from xcms) plus centroided MS/MS scans, and you need to associate each MS2 spectrum with the correct feature to enable MS/MS spectral similarity or networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-scan-to-feature-association))
- **ms2-similarity-network-annotation**: Use when annotating untargeted LC-MS/MS features beyond spectral-library hits by propagating annotations through a knowledge-guided MS2 similarity network connecting reaction-paired metabolites with similar MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-similarity-network-annotation))
- **ms2-similarity-network-tool-comparison**: Use when a feature table carries network-propagated level 3 annotations (e.g. MetDNA3 output on Orbitrap data) and you need to quantify how many annotations independent in-silico tools support. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-similarity-network-tool-comparison))
- **ms2-spectra-construction-from-peak-lists**: Use when you have raw MS2 peak lists (paired m/z and intensity vectors) with known precursor m/z values and need to assemble them into Spectra objects for comparison or conversion, rather than reading from mzML/MGF files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-construction-from-peak-lists))
- **ms2-spectra-de-novo-structure-generation**: Use when you have positive-mode MS2 spectra (MGF or SIRIUS .ms) with no library match, need de novo candidate structures, or want to evaluate whether de novo candidates rival database-search annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-de-novo-structure-generation))
- **ms2-spectra-deconvolution**: Use when raw DDA or SWATH-DIA MS/MS spectra contain co-eluting or chimeric fragment ions that must be reassigned to their precursor ions before compound annotation or functional interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-deconvolution))
- **ms2-spectra-parsing-and-loading**: Use when when beginning a MEMO analysis workflow with raw or unaligned MS2 spectra files and needing to extract fragmentation data and precursor information before counting MS2 peaks and neutral losses to generate sample fingerprints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectra-parsing-and-loading) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when starting the MAW workflow on raw untargeted tandem MS data in .mzML format and you need MS2 pre-processing plus spectral dereplication outputs that feed SIRIUS or MetFrag candidate generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-preprocessing))
- **ms2-spectra-retrieval-and-representative-selection**: Use when you have raw HRMS data (mzML) plus a compound or mass list and need first-look prescreening: retrieve EICs and MS2 scans within an m/z tolerance, QC each MS1 parent, and pick a representative MS2 spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-retrieval-and-representative-selection))
- **ms2-spectra-source-attribution**: Use when combining MS2 spectra from paired DDA and DIA-(SWATH) acquisitions of the same sample set and you need each spectrum record in the exported MGF labeled with its DDA or DIA provenance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectra-source-attribution))
- **ms2-spectra-tic-filtering**: Use when after concatenating replicate MS/MS spectra for each precursor feature (m/z and retention time), use this skill when you have multiple replicate scans per feature and need to reduce spectral count while preserving the highest-intensity, most-reliable spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectra-tic-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-clustering**: Use when tens of millions of MS/MS spectra (tens to hundreds of GB) must be merged into consensus spectra before peptide identification and CPU-only clustering tools like MS-Cluster, spectra-cluster, or MaRaCluster are too slow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-clustering))
- **ms2-spectral-clustering-and-cleaning**: Use when processing raw LC–MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data (e.g. mzML/MGF per-sample MS2 files from a feature-detection step) and you need to consolidate redundant MS2 spectra into clean consensus spectra for downstream molecular networking, library identification, and bioactivity correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-clustering-and-cleaning))
- **ms2-spectral-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when repeated MS/MS acquisitions in DDA metabolomics data must be de-duplicated by clustering, and the clustering quality (purity vs completeness N10) of Falcon, MaRaCluster, or MS-Cluster must be compared without metabolite ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-clustering-benchmarking))
- **ms2-spectral-database-curation-characterization**: Use when a local annotation tool ships a built-in MS2 spectral library (e.g. Met-ID's FMP-10 database) and you must quantify its contents — number of derivatized forms, parent standards, CID energy range — before trusting downstream identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-database-curation-characterization))
- **ms2-spectral-deconvolution-and-annotation**: Use when you have acquired LC-IMS-MS/MS data (or equivalent multidimensional MS/MS acquisition) in mzML or mzML.gz format and need to disambiguate overlapping fragmentation spectra arising from co-eluting or co-drifting precursor ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-deconvolution-and-annotation) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-dissimilarity-scoring**: Use when you have MS2 fragmentation spectra from multiple metabolomics samples and need to identify samples with unusual spectral profiles that may indicate novel chemistry, independent of feature abundance or annotation status. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-dissimilarity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-entropy-similarity-networking**: Use when LC-HRMS MS2 peak lists exist for enzymatic-assay-derived candidate metabolites and their parent xenobiotics, and candidates must be assigned to parents by spectral similarity rather than reference-library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-entropy-similarity-networking))
- **ms2-spectral-feature-extraction**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS metabolomics data in MGF format and need to prepare it for Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) topic modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-interpretation**: Use when you have extracted an MS1 feature table (from XCMS or custom CSV with m/z, retention time, and intensity columns) and one or more DDA mzXML files from the same or related LC-MS runs, and you need to annotate features with MS2 spectral data and match them against a standard spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-library-aggregation-across-public-databases**: Use when building or auditing a compound-identification spectral library that must merge spectra from many public MS2 sources with deduplication and unique-compound counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-aggregation-across-public-databases))
- **ms2-spectral-library-annotation**: Use when MS1 features from an LC-MS metabolomics/exposomics run (e.g., urine RP(+) DDA mzXML data) need compound-level identification by matching DDA MS2 spectra against an in-house .msp standard library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-annotation--230e4ce682d854b5))
- **ms2-spectral-library-annotation**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS (DDA or DIA) spectra have been processed to features and you need compound-level annotation of MS2 peaks via spectral library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-annotation--45dfd7483a2c9b7b))
- **ms2-spectral-library-annotation-rate-analysis**: Use when you have FBMN/IIMN outputs (feature table .csv, .mgf MS2 summary, IIN edge .csv files) and need to compute annotation rates and the gain from propagating spectral library matches over ion identity neighbors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-annotation-rate-analysis))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching**: Use when centroided LC-MS/MS mzML data (DDA or SWATH-DIA) has yielded consensus MS2 spectra per MS1 feature and compounds must be identified by spectral library matching using MS2, MS1, retention-time and isotope similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching--b28b446a8370a5f5))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching**: Use when annotating untargeted LC-MS metabolomics peaks where MS2 spectra (.mgf or .mzML) are available alongside an m/z feature table and metabolite-level annotation confidence is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching--266d299c58124d35))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching**: Use when mapped empirical compounds carry experimental MS2 spectra and putative identifications need reference-library support; i.e., after map_ms2 has associated empCpds with MS2 peaks and Level 1 (MS1) annotation is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching--c3c81df257b10cdf))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching**: Use when tissue MS2 spectra (e.g. from mzML files) need to be compared against reference MS2 spectra of chemical standards — such as FMP-10-derivatized neurotransmitters — to annotate on-tissue metabolites or quantify bin-size sensitivity of similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching--925c405422c4706d))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching**: Use when untargeted metabolomics LC-MS/MS data (e.g., xcms peak-derived spectra or MZmine-exported MGF files) needs compound-level MS2 annotation against a reference spectral library such as HMDB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching--cc7a9557ed76ead1))
- **ms2-spectral-library-matching-post-denoising**: Use when after frequency-based denoising of MS/MS spectra, when you need to validate that denoising improves metabolite identifications and quantify the trade-off between signal retention and annotation confidence. Specifically: (1) you have denoised MS/MS spectra from replicate measurements; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-matching-post-denoising) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-library-search**: Use when an LC-MS/MS lipidomics feature has a measured MS2 peak list (m/z + intensity + precursor m/z) and the goal is library-based annotation of lipid species, chain composition, or C=C double-bond position. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-library-search))
- **ms2-spectral-match-annotation-prioritization**: Use when you have a Qemistree hierarchy and merged feature data and need chemical class labels (e.g., acyl-carnitines, flavonoids) to trace class-specific subtrees against sample metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-match-annotation-prioritization))
- **ms2-spectral-quality-assessment**: Use when you need to quantify how 'good' tandem MS spectra are—e.g. comparing DDA vs DIA(SWATH) vs DaDIA MS2 quality—by scoring each spectrum against a standard spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-quality-assessment))
- **ms2-spectral-similarity-computation**: Use when you have two or more LC-MS/MS datasets (in mzML, mzXML, or MGF format) and need to quantify their overall spectral content similarity—typical scenarios include data quality control, species identification, molecular phylogenetics, or proteome comparison across samples, cell lines, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-similarity-computation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-similarity-network-analysis**: Use when an aligned LC-MS/MS feature table (.csv), consensus MS2 spectra (.mgf), and ion-identity supplementary edges are available and network connectivity or feature redundancy across adducts and isotopes is the bottleneck. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-similarity-network-analysis))
- **ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have MS1 features with MS2 spectra and a reaction-linked feature-pair table, and must filter network edges by spectral similarity before metabolite annotation propagation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring--eab75d9a445dbcf3))
- **ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have an experimental MS2 peak list (m/z, intensity pairs) and need a per-pair similarity score against reference metabolite spectra to support compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring--70e5ed9472f67d8f))
- **ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring**: Use when after temporal intensity profile correlation and exact mass difference refinement have identified candidate ion-species pairs in direct-injection plasma ionization MS data (e.g., DBDI-MS, DBDI-FT-ICR-MS). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectral-similarity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectral-vectorization-memo**: Use when you need to compare metabolomics samples by their overall MS2 fragmentation content rather than aligned features — e.g. chemodiverse samples with poor feature overlap, strong RT shifts, or different LC/MS methods — or to flag spectrally dissimilar extracts for novelty prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectral-vectorization-memo))
- **ms2-spectrum-annotation**: Use when centroided ddMS2 mzML data must be inspected at the spectrum level — e.g., to verify whether PFAS diagnostic fragments or fragment mass differences were detected for a prioritized feature before assigning a suspect hit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectrum-annotation))
- **ms2-spectrum-clustering**: Use when processing raw MS2 peak lists (e.g. .mzXML) for classic molecular networking and a reduced set of representative consensus spectra is needed instead of redundant duplicate scans. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectrum-clustering))
- **ms2-spectrum-clustering-mgf-generation**: Use when a processed LC-MS/MS feature table (OpenMS consensusXML) must be exported for GNPS Feature-Based Molecular Networking or Ion Identity Molecular Networking, i.e. you need an MGF plus quantification and metadata files for a GNPS upload. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectrum-clustering-mgf-generation))
- **ms2-spectrum-collection-from-data-dependent-acquisition**: Use when you have DDA LC-MS/MS raw data (mzML format) with detected chromatographic peaks at a target m/z value and retention time window, and you need to build a high-confidence MS2 consensus spectrum for that peak to match against reference spectra (e.g., Metlin, GNPS). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectrum-collection-from-data-dependent-acquisition) · 3 copies)
- **ms2-spectrum-consolidation**: Use when after peak picking, sample alignment, and isotopologue/adduct grouping are complete, and you have DDA-MS2 scans associated with grouped feature ions. - **ms2-spectrum-consolidation**: Use when after sample alignment and feature grouping steps in untargeted LC-MS workflows, when you have DDA-mode raw files with both MS1 and MS2 scans and need to link tandem mass spectra to quantified features for annotation and structural characterization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectrum-consolidation) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectrum-count-normalization**: Use when comparing untargeted metabolomics feature tables produced with different centWave parameter sets (AutoTuner- vs IPO-derived) and you need to test whether a feature subset is depleted for MS2 spectra after controlling for subset size. - **ms2-spectrum-extraction-and-consensus-building**: Use when when you have DDA LC-MS/MS data (mzML format) with identified chromatographic peaks at a specific m/z (e.g., 304.1131) and multiple MS2 spectra fragmented from that precursor, and you need to produce a single high-confidence MS2 spectrum for comparison against reference databases (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectrum-extraction-and-consensus-building) · 4 copies)
- **ms2-spectrum-feature-extraction**: Use when you have DDA (data-dependent acquisition) LC-MS/MS data with MS2 spectra and want to discover metabolic features that may be missed by MS1-only peak picking, or when you need an alternative feature extraction workflow that leverages fragmentation patterns to identify true metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectrum-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **ms2-spectrum-format-preparation**: Use when you have raw or unstructured MS2 spectral data (from untargeted tandem mass spectrometry experiments) and plan to run MS2MP inference for KEGG pathway prediction. - **ms2-spectrum-input-encoding**: Use when raw or profile MS2 spectra (mzML for prediction, HDF5 for training) must be converted into peak-embedding plus positional-encoding input sequences for a transformer that predicts lipid species tokens. - **ms2-spectrum-peak-filtering-by-training-set-membership**: Use when when you have raw MS2 spectra (m/z and intensity pairs) and need to compute a Probability Product Kernel score or other fragmentation-based similarity metric against a training dataset of known spectra (e.g., GNPS library spectra with structural annotations). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ms2-spectrum-peak-filtering-by-training-set-membership) · 2 copies)

- **ms2-spectrum-peak-normalization**: Use when MS2 spectra in .mzML format must be normalized and low-intensity peaks filtered prior to cosine-similarity dereplication against spectral databases like GNPS, HMDB, or MassBank. - **ms2-spectrum-peak-selection-and-deconvolution**: Use when MS2 spectra come from a miniaturized, low-resolution mass spectrometer and must be reduced to a compact, error-corrected peak list before feeding a de novo structure-prediction model such as TeFT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectrum-peak-selection-and-deconvolution))

- **ms2-spectrum-quality-criteria-checking**: Use when building a large-scale MS/MS training corpus (e.g. from GeMS-style mining of 250,000 LC–MS/MS experiments) and redundant or low-quality spectra must be filtered into nested quality tiers. - **ms2-spectrum-quality-filtering**: Use when building IIMN-based propagated spectral libraries from ion identity networks where only high-confident, high-quality MS2 consensus spectra should become library entries. - **ms2-spectrum-similarity-networking**: Use when LC-HRMS/PRM spectra from biotransformation assays (e.g. human liver S9 incubations of pesticides) contain metabolites with no reference library spectra, and top-down annotation is needed via spectral similarity clustering. - **ms2-spectrum-subformula-assignment**: Use when performing database-independent, de novo chemical formula assignment from MS/MS spectra where fragmentation trees (SIRIUS) are too slow or unavailable, and you need peak-to-subformula mappings to feed an energy-based formula scorer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2-spectrum-subformula-assignment))

- **ms2-vs-ms3-filter-cutoff-optimization**: Use when filtering isobarically-labeled (iTRAQ/TMT/TMTpro) peptide quantifications for co-isolation interference and you must choose which IsobaricQuant QC score and threshold to apply, differently for MS2- and MS3-level data. - **ms2deep-score-embedding-generation**: Use when HR-MS/MS spectra (e.g., MGF inference spectra of Glycyrrhiza uralensis root or Paulownia tomentosa fruit extracts) need MS2DeepScore structure-informed embeddings as classifier input in AnnoMe. - **ms2deepscore-candidate-preselection-and-reranking**: Use when annotating MS2 query spectra against a large library (e.g. GNPS) and both exact matches and analogues are needed, with a fast, scalable alternative to modified cosine score-based searching. - **ms2deepscore-embedding-generation**: Use when a set of HR-MS/MS spectra (e.g. MSnLib MGF files plus in-house BOKU database spectra, or inference extracts) must be converted into MS2DeepScore structure-informed embeddings before AnnoMe classifier training or prediction. - **ms2deepscore-pretrained-model-inference**: Use when MS/MS spectra in .mgf format need pairwise spectral similarity scores and no suitable MS/MS library search target exists, so pretrained machine-learned scoring (ms2deepscore via MS2Query) is required. - **ms2deepscore-similarity-scoring**: Use when ranking GNPS-style MS2 library spectra against query spectra without precursor m/z preselection, e.g. selecting top-2000 candidates for random-forest re-ranking or comparing spectral similarity methods for exact-match benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2deepscore-similarity-scoring--9bf7d126a99082c6))
- **ms2deepscore-similarity-scoring**: Use when tandem MS/MS spectra must be compared across ionization modes (e.g., a Rutin positive-mode vs negative-mode pair), or when cosine (0.0) and modified cosine (0.3) scores underreport true chemical similarity of structurally identical compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ms2deepscore-similarity-scoring--b72aa39dbbe946f5))

- **ms2lda-feature-annotation**: Use when after creating a GNPS mass spectral molecular network and running an MS2LDA experiment, use this skill when you want to identify and visualize which substructural motifs (Mass2Motifs) are shared across clustered spectra, particularly to highlight fragmentation pattern similarities between. - **ms2lda-mass2motif-mapping**: Use when annotated MS1/MS2 peak features should be grouped into GNPS FBMN Molecular Families and MS2LDA Mass2Motif metabolite sets for PLAGE-based activity scoring in PALS. - **ms2lda-mass2motif-threshold-filtering**: Use when MS2LDA motif summary tables (e.g. MotifFS2.csv) must be thresholded prior to mapping ring/tail-type Mass2Motifs onto GNPS molecular network nodes and edges. - **ms2lda-motif-to-network-mapping**: Use when when you have a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based) and corresponding MS2LDA experiment results, and you want to annotate network nodes with discovered substructural motifs to support structural elucidation and chemical family interpretation. - **msi-intensity-normalization**: Use when raw MSI data (e.g., rat brain sections, MALDI) must be converted into preprocessed 2D pixel×ion intensity matrices—e.g., before DeepION representation learning or other ion-colocalization tasks requiring uniform [0,1] intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-intensity-normalization))
- **msi-ion-image-augmentation**: Use when training a contrastive encoder on MSI ion images and you need augmentation pairs that reflect MSI priors — Poisson ion counts, missing values, or isotope-ion relationships — rather than generic image transforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ion-image-augmentation))
- **msi-ion-image-extraction-and-overlay-visualization**: Use when you have a preprocessed imzML dataset and need to extract an ion image at a specific m/z, or to overlay that ion image on a co-registered H&E stain for spatial localization review. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ion-image-extraction-and-overlay-visualization))

- **msi-level1-authentic-standard-validation**: Use when you have MSI level 1 authentic-standard MS/MS identifications and need to quantify how accurately an in silico annotation tool (e.g., CSI:FingerID) ranks the true structure among its candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-level1-authentic-standard-validation))
- **msi-matrix-reshaping-and-shape-validation**: Use when raw MSI matrices must be exported as a two-dimensional pixel-by-ion matrix plus a one-dimensional peak list and the horizontal/vertical pixel shape passed to DeepION's --input_shape must be verified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-matrix-reshaping-and-shape-validation))
- **msi-metabolite-annotation**: Use when you have preprocessed MSI data with a measured m/z column and need to assign metabolite names and adduct forms by matching against an organ- and ion-mode-specific metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-metabolite-annotation))
- **msi-metabolite-database-interpretation**: Use when metabolite annotations from ambient MSI (e.g., AFADESI-MSI of mouse body sections) must be organized into per-organ membership/enrichment sets and compared across organs, ionization modes, or mouse strains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-metabolite-database-interpretation))
- **msi-metadata-curation-and-storage**: Use when after generating a 3D pixel array (shape n+1, y, x) from raw line-scan MSI data via MSIGen's get_image_data() call, curate and validate the accompanying JSON metadata file before visualization or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-metadata-curation-and-storage) · 2 copies)
- **msi-metadata-harmonization-across-image-stacks**: Use when you have processed and quantified MSI data from one or more imzML files in LipidQMap and need to export them as a unified, standards-compliant HDF5 container that preserves feature-by-pixel intensity matrices, per-feature lipid annotations (m/z, lipid class, adduct, neutral ID), per-pixel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-metadata-harmonization-across-image-stacks))
- **msi-mz-matching**: Use when a preprocessed MSI feature list (measured m/z in column 1) must be annotated against an organ-specific metabolite database (metabolite name + theoretical m/z in specified adduct columns) with a controlled ppm tolerance. - **msi-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a centroid-mode MSI peak matrix (e.g. rMSIproc .zip format) and need dimensionality reduction to visually compare data quality before and after a peak-removal step such as matrix-related signal removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **msi-peak-annotation**: Use when you have processed MSI data (peak matrix and spatial coordinates) from matrix-assisted laser desorption/ionization (MALDI) or silver-assisted laser desorption/ionization (AgLDI) experiments, and you need to computationally distinguish matrix ions from analyte ions before downstream feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-peak-annotation))
- **msi-peak-filtering-pooling**: Use when raw MALDI-MSI section data (e.g. rat brain, positive or negative ion mode) must be converted into a validated pixel-by-ion intensity matrix with a paired peak list before running DeepION COL/ISO or similar ion-image representation models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-filtering-pooling))
- **msi-peak-intensity-extraction**: Use when you have MSI intensity data exported from commercial software (MSiReader, SCiLS) or Cardinal processing pipelines as plain-text CSV files or Cardinal MSProcessedImagingExperiment/MSContinuousImagingExperiment objects, and you need to prepare it for mass-difference tabulation and adduct. - **msi-peak-matrix-processing**: Use when raw MSI spectral data (imzML in profile mode) must be converted into a centroid-mode peak matrix and processed dataset suitable for matrix-related peak annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-matrix-processing))
- **msi-peak-pair-mass-difference-calculation**: Use when you have binned MSI peak data (CSV from SCiLS/MSiReader or an msimat object) and need to enumerate candidate adduct ion pairs by mass difference before adduct annotation and spatial correlation. - **msi-peak-picked-intensity-analysis**: Use when segmenting peak-picked MSI pixel-by-feature intensity data (e.g. processed imzML loaded into Cardinal) into spatially coherent tissue regions and selecting the number of segments by scanning a regularization parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-picked-intensity-analysis))
- **msi-peak-picking-alignment**: Use when raw MALDI MSI sections must be converted into a common peak list and a [X*Y,P] ion-intensity matrix with normalized intensities before a representation model such as DeepION is trained. - **msi-peak-table-import-msimat**: Use when starting a mass2adduct adduct-analysis workflow on MALDI-MSI data and you need to load exported peak tables or processed Cardinal objects into the `msimat` class before calling massdiff, adductMatch, or corrPairsMSI. - **msi-pixel-classification-by-ifm-marker**: Use when co-registered MALDI MSI and immunofluorescence microscopy data are available and you need to assign lipids to marker-defined cell populations (e.g. Ly6G+ neutrophils in UPEC-infected bladder) via supervised pixel classification. - **msi-pixel-filtering**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table matrix (pixels x m/z ion columns) contains off-tissue/background pixels that must be removed before downstream clustering, e.g. when an intensity image shows clearly separable background signal. - **msi-pixel-to-spot-binning**: Use when pairing pixel-resolution MALDI imaging mass spectrometry data with spot-based ST data (e.g. Visium) from the same tissue section, and a shared spot-level object is needed for multi-omic integration. - **msi-principal-component-analysis**: Use when the input is an MSI dataset (imzML or Analyze 7.5) and the goal is unsupervised exploratory analysis to discover regions of interest, or when benchmarking the runtime cost of PCA on raw versus pre-processed/peak-picked spectra. - **msi-spectrum-binary-classification-inference**: Use when you have raw MALDI FT-ICR MSI spectra (e.g., 85,062 m/z variables) with ground-truth normal/tumor labels and a trained massNet model, and need probabilistic binary pixel-level classification plus ROC AUC, accuracy, confusion matrix, and runtime.
